### 写作规则

- **[EMNLP 2026]** Limitations 章节只需要写一小段，覆盖 3 个点左右就可以了，不需要写得很长

### 审稿规则

- **[EMNLP 2026]** “EMNLP 2026 通过 ARR 统一审稿，Main/Findings 不分开投稿（不是两个Track），Long/Short 也走同一审稿系统，Overall Assessment的评分标准如下：5 = Consider for Award（约前2.5%），4.5 = Borderline Award，4 = Conference（可被主会议接收），3.5 = Borderline Conference，3 = Findings（可被Findings接收），2.5 = Borderline Findings，2 = Resubmit next cycle（需大幅修改，可在下一周期完成），1.5 = Resubmit after next cycle（需大幅修改，无法在下一周期完成），1 = Do not resubmit（需完全重做或与*ACL无关）”
- **[EMNLP 2026]** EMNLP 2026 restricts long paper submissions to a strict 8-page limit for main text, with unlimited space for references. Mandatory limitations and optional ethical considerations do not count towards this page limit, provided they do not contain excessive additional content.

> 以上规则由用户配置，优先级等同于硬约束。违反会被 Reviewer 标记。

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. 18	
19	# 论文写作风格规范
20	
21	> 核心哲学：**论文是一个有说服力的故事，不是实验报告。** 审稿人在几十秒内判断论文价值——如果 Abstract 和 Introduction 读起来像实验日志，论文就已经失败了。
22	
23	---
24	
25	## 一、总体写作哲学
26	
27	### 1.1 故事驱动，不是数据堆砌
28	
29	- 论文必须以"故事线"为核心展开：问题 → 洞察 → 方法 → 验证
30	- 禁止大量实验数据堆砌——每个数字出现必须服务于叙事推进
31	- 附录内容必须在正文中有明确引用且融入叙事，禁止简单堆砌
32	- 如果附录数据对核心叙事有重大影响，根据附录数据调整论文 framing
33	
34	### 1.2 主线统一性
35	
36	- 当 idea 在研究过程中发生变化后，**必须重审全文逻辑**
37	- 常见问题：Introduction 讲的故事 A，Method 做的是故事 B，Results 又在讲故事 C
38	- 修复方式：从最终实验结论反推，重写 Introduction 的问题定义和动机
39	- 每次 idea 调整后检查：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否形成一条连贯主线
40	
41	### 1.3 专有名词必须有定义
42	
43	- 首次出现的专有名词/缩写必须给出完整定义和解释
44	- Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义（如 LLM = Large Language Model）
45	- 不能假设读者知道你自己定义的术语——论文从来没解释过的概念不能直接使用
46	- 通用规则：如果一个非标准术语第一次出现时没有括号定义，就是写作 bug
47	
48	### 1.4 写作前必读同类优秀论文
49	
50	- 每个章节动笔前，找 **2-3 篇目标会议近 1-2 年的高分论文**作为风格参考——同类问题或同类方法皆可
51	- 重点学：
52	  - Abstract 怎么用最少篇幅讲完故事
53	  - Introduction 怎么从大问题切入到具体贡献
54	  - Related Work 怎么 positioning 自己（不是 survey）
55	  - Table/Figure 怎么压缩信息密度
56	- **不是抄袭**，是学习同行的故事节奏和审美——不同子领域叙事节奏差异很大
57	- 实操：Paper Agent 可以从 `literature_map.yaml` 中挑 `role=foundation` 或 `role=direct-competitor` 的论文，把 `{title, venue, year, abstract}` 附在 Worker 的 task_prompt 中作为概念参考；如需学习写作风格本身，让 Worker 通过 WebSearch 取得论文链接并 fetch 全文
58	
59	---
60	
61	## 二、Abstract 写作规范
62	
63	### 2.1 四个必答问题（150-250 words）
64	
65	Abstract 必须在很短的篇幅内清楚回答：
66	1. **研究什么问题** — 1-2 句话交代背景和痛点
67	2. **为什么重要** — 现有方法的不足（不用写太多）
68	3. **本文提出什么** — 核心贡献的关键机制（简要，不堆砌细节）
69	4. **结果说明什么** — 用具体实验结论支撑贡献
70	
71	### 2.2 禁忌
72	
73	- **禁止堆砌数据**：Abstract 不是 Results 的缩写。最多引用 2-3 个关键数字，要服务于 claim
74	- **禁止过度宣传**：不说 "revolutionary"、"groundbreaking"
75	- **禁止写成 Introduction 的缩写**：Abstract 是独立阅读单元
76	- **禁止术语堆积**：如果审稿人几十秒内判断不出价值，就失败了
77	- **缩写必须定义**：Abstract 中首次出现的缩写必须展开
78	
79	### 2.3 审稿人视角检验
80	
81	写完后用这个标准自检：审稿人能否在 30 秒内判断——
82	- 问题是否重要？
83	- 方法是否清楚？
84	- 贡献是否可信？
85	- 结果是否有说服力？
86	
87	---
88	
89	## 三、Introduction 写作规范
90	
91	### 3.1 完整叙事线（5 段结构）
92	
93	Introduction 需要从一个清晰、重要的问题切入，逐步把读者带到核心贡献：
94	
95	1. **研究背景**（1-2 段）：大模型在某类能力/应用中已取得进展，但仍存在一个关键瓶颈
96	2. **问题重要性**（1 段）：结合真实应用、已有方法局限或当前趋势，说明这不是小问题
97	3. **现有方法不足**（1 段）：系统梳理为什么不够（依赖更大模型/更多数据/更复杂推理/更高成本，或只在特定 benchmark 有效）
98	4. **本文核心想法**（1 段）：我们观察到什么、提出了什么方法、为什么能解决上述问题
99	5. **贡献总结**（列表）：清楚列出方法贡献、实验贡献和分析贡献
100	
101	### 3.2 整体叙事结构
102	
103	```
104	现有进展 → 关键缺口 → 本文洞察 → 方法概述 → 实验证据 → 贡献总结
105	```
106	
107	### 3.3 禁忌
108	
109	- **禁止泛泛介绍**：不要开头就写 "LLMs have shown remarkable capabilities..."，要从具体问题切入
110	- **禁止缺少过渡**：每段之间必须有逻辑连接
111	- **禁止实验报告风格**：Introduction 不是 "我们做了 X 实验得到了 Y 结果" 的罗列
112	- 每条 Contribution 必须有对应的 section/table 支撑
113	- Contribution 列表中不能出现未在前文铺垫过的概念
114	
115	---
116	
117	## 四、Related Work 写作规范
118	
119	### 4.1 目标
120	
121	让审稿人相信：作者理解这个领域，知道自己的方法站在什么位置，清楚相对已有工作的真实增量在哪里。
122	
123	### 4.2 结构要求
124	
125	- 把已有工作分成几个清晰的主题（如模型训练、推理增强、工具调用、数据构建、评测等）
126	- 每类简要说明：已有方法做了什么、解决了哪些问题、和本文的关系
127	- 帮助读者理解：哪些是本文直接继承的，哪些方法不同但解决相似问题，哪些留下了本文要解决的缺口
128	
129	### 4.3 禁忌
130	
131	- **禁止论文堆砌**：不要写成 "A proposed X. B proposed Y. C proposed Z." 的列表
132	- **目标不是展示读了多少论文**，而是做归纳和比较
133	- Contribution claim（"To our knowledge, no prior work..."）应放在 Introduction，不在 Related Work
134	
135	### 4.4 引用充足度
136	
137	- 引用必须覆盖：**直接相关方法、baseline 论文、评测 benchmark 论文、理论基础**
138	- 任何一类缺失，审稿人会立刻看出来调研不充分
139	- 引用密度参照目标会议近年录用论文的量级——明显偏少意味着 Related Work 调研不足
140	
141	---
142	
143	## 五、去 AI 写作风格（硬规则）
144	
145	以下模式是 AI 生成文本的典型标志，审稿人一眼能看出。**必须全部消除。**
146	
147	### 5.1 严禁破折号（最显著的 AI 痕迹）
148	
149	**这是 AI 写作最容易被识别的标志，审稿人最敏感。单列为最高优先级硬规则。**
150	
151	- 解释性 em-dash（—，U+2014）和 en-dash（–，U+2013）必须**全部删除**
152	- 替代方式：用逗号、括号或拆分为两个句子
153	- 强制检查命令：
154	  ```bash
155	  grep -n "—" paper/*.tex     # em-dash，每处人工判断是否必要
156	  grep -n " – " paper/*.tex   # 解释性 en-dash，几乎都应删除
157	  ```
158	- 例外（允许保留）：表格分隔符、数字范围（`2020–2024`、`pp. 12–18`）、专有名词（`Newton–Raphson`）
159	
160	### 5.2 段落与句式
161	
162	- **禁止过度分段**：不需要每 2-3 句就换段，自然段 5-8 句是正常的
163	- **减少关系从句**（which, that, who）：能用简洁直接的句子就不用从句
164	- **禁止 -ing 短语结尾**：如 "..., highlighting the applicability of..." / "..., demonstrating the effectiveness of..."。这是 AI 写作最典型的标志之一
165	- **禁止连续使用 "Furthermore" / "Moreover" / "Additionally"**：一段内最多用一个过渡词
166	- **避免 "It is worth noting that..." / "Notably, ..."**：直接写要说的内容
167	
168	### 5.3 用词黑名单
169	
170	- 避免 "delve into" / "leverage" / "utilize" / "facilitate" — 用 "explore" / "use" / "enable"
171	- 避免 "a myriad of" / "a plethora of" — 用 "many" / "various"
172	- 避免 "in the realm of" / "in the context of" — 直接说
173	- 避免 "paradigm shift" / "novel" 过度使用
174	
175	### 5.4 校对清单（4 步）
176	
177	1. 全文 proofread 语法和拼写
178	2. 检查所有代词/指示词（this, that, it）是否指代明确
179	3. **`grep "—"` + `grep " – "` 全文清零**（删除所有解释性 dash，§5.1）
180	4. `grep "ing,"` 模式——大部分 -ing 分词短语需要改写
181	
182	---
183	
184	## 六、图表与视觉规范
185	
186	### 6.1 知识密度原则
187	
188	- **图表的信息必须紧凑**——不能为了美观而留大量白空间
189	- 一张图如果只传达一个简单信息，要么缩小要么合并到其他图中
190	- 能用 inline text 说清的不要占一整个 Figure
191	
192	### 6.2 表格规范
193	
194	- 表格和正文文字不能视觉重叠（检查 column 宽度和 alignment）
195	- 小数精度全表统一
196	- 最优加粗、次优下划线
197	- Caption 必须自包含
198	
199	### 6.3 图片规范
200	
201	- 数值标签不能被线、bar、点遮住——用白色背景或偏移标签
202	- 图中字体 >= 7pt（打印后仍可读）
203	- 栏宽约束需匹配目标 venue 模板要求
204	- 概念图/流程图要有信息量，不要画一个 trivial 的 pipeline 然后占半页
205	
206	### 6.4 空间效率
207	
208	- 顶会正文页数严格受限（具体页数查目标会议官网），每 cm² 都是宝贵资源
209	- 优先使用 `\subfigure` 合并相关图
210	- Table 能 inline 就不要单独浮动
211	- 图表占比与其对叙事的贡献成正比
212	
213	### 6.5 页面节奏
214	
215	好论文的页面有节奏感——text、equation、figure 交替出现，读者视觉不疲劳：
216	
217	- **text-equation-figure 三重奏**：重要概念用"文字解释 → 公式定义 → 图表直觉"三步展开
218	- 连续 2 页纯文本（无 figure/equation/table）→ 穿插一个 display item 打破节奏
219	- 连续 2 个 figure 页（整页被 float 占满）→ 至少隔 1 段文字再放下一个 float
220	- Section heading 不孤悬页底（heading 后至少跟 2 行正文，否则强制分页）
221	- 最后一页内容 ≥ 2/3 页面高度（太空显得凑数，太满放不下 references）
222	
223	### 6.6 表格 LaTeX 规范
224	
225	**Booktabs 标准（硬规则）**：
226	- 用 `\toprule` / `\midrule` / `\bottomrule`，禁止 `\hline`，禁止竖线 `|`
227	- 用 `\centering` 不用 `\begin{center}`（后者多加垂直空白）
228	- 最优结果加粗（`\textbf{}`），次优下划线（`\underline{}`）+ 方向箭头 `↑`/`↓` 标明指标方向
229	- 小数精度全表统一（如全部保留 1 位或 2 位）
230	- Caption 在表格上方（`\caption` 在 `\begin{tabular}` 之前）
231	
232	**宽度决策树**：
233	- ≤5 列 → 单栏宽度（`\columnwidth`）
234	- 6–8 列 → 全文宽度（`\textwidth`，双栏模板用 `table*`）
235	- >8 列 → 拆成两张表或移入 appendix
236	
237	---
238	
239	## 七、投稿前通用准备
240	
241	### 7.1 模板必须官方（硬规则）
242	
243	- LaTeX 模板**必须从目标会议官网下载**（CallForPapers / Author Instructions 页面）
244	- **不要用**：
245	  - 网上他人转载的复制版本（可能被改过 margin / 行号设置）
246	  - 上一年的模板（参数可能变：字体、margin、行号、anonymous 选项等）
247	  - 组里学长留的旧版本（来源不明、无法追溯）
248	- 风险：
249	  - 样式不符合 → desk reject
250	  - margin 不对 → 被审稿人质疑诚信
251	  - reference 格式错 → 被认为不规范
252	- 流程：先查会议官网 templates → 找不到再 `WebSearch "{venue} {year} latex template official"` → 仍找不到汇报 Director（不要用就近年版本顶替）
253	- `\documentclass[]{}` 选项必须与目标 venue + 阶段（review / camera-ready）一致
254	
255	### 7.2 投稿要求逐项核对
256	
257	- 目标会议如要求 Checklist / Ethical Statement / Broader Impact Statement / Reproducibility Statement，必须**认真填每一项**，不要全写 N/A
258	- 页数限制：查目标会议官网
259	- 匿名化要求：review 版 vs camera-ready 版本设置不同
260	- 行号开启（review 版常需）
261	- 字体大小、margin 不得修改 sty 文件
262	
263	### 7.3 最终修改 10 项检查流程
264	
265	提交前必须逐一检查：
266	1. 所有最新实验结果已全部录入
267	2. 根据实验结果将叙事调至最优状态
268	3. 附录所有内容在正文中被引用且融入叙事
269	4. 无数据堆砌（每个数字服务于叙事推进）
270	5. Abstract 和 Introduction 具有吸引力（不是实验报告风格）
271	6. 全文主线统一（idea 变化后各章节逻辑一致）
272	7. 所有专有名词有定义
273	8. 无 AI 风格标志（dash、-ing phrases、过度分段）
274	9. 图表知识密度高、无视觉遮挡
275	10. Reference 覆盖直接相关方法、baseline、benchmark、理论基础
276	
````

      "content": "---\nname: paper-writing-norms\ndescription: 顶会级别论文写作风格规范——章节写法（Abstract/Intro/Related Work）、去 AI 风格、视觉密度、叙事哲学。Paper Agent 和 Worker 写论文章节时必须加载。\nallowed-tools: [Read, Write, Edit, Glob, Grep]\nscope: global\nversion: 2\npriority: 9\ntags:\n  - writing\n  - paper\n  - style\n  - norms\nroles:\n  - agent\n  - worker\n  - paper_agent\n---\n\n# 论文写作风格规范\n\n> 核心哲学：**论文是一个有说服力的故事，不是实验报告。** 审稿人在几十秒内判断论文价值——如果 Abstract 和 Introduction 读起来像实验日志，论文就已经失败了。\n\n---\n\n## 一、总体写作哲学\n\n### 1.1 故事驱动，不是数据堆砌\n\n- 论文必须以\"故事线\"为核心展开：问题 → 洞察 → 方法 → 验证\n- 禁止大量实验数据堆砌——每个数字出现必须服务于叙事推进\n- 附录内容必须在正文中有明确引用且融入叙事，禁止简单堆砌\n- 如果附录数据对核心叙事有重大影响，根据附录数据调整论文 framing\n\n### 1.2 主线统一性\n\n- 当 idea 在研究过程中发生变化后，**必须重审全文逻辑**\n- 常见问题：Introduction 讲的故事 A，Method 做的是故事 B，Results 又在讲故事 C\n- 修复方式：从最终实验结论反推，重写 Introduction 的问题定义和动机\n- 每次 idea 调整后检查：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否形成一条连贯主线\n\n### 1.3 专有名词必须有定义\n\n- 首次出现的专有名词/缩写必须给出完整定义和解释\n- Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义（如 LLM = Large Language Model）\n- 不能假设读者知道你自己定义的术语——论文从来没解释过的概念不能直接使用\n- 通用规则：如果一个非标准术语第一次出现时没有括号定义，就是写作 bug\n\n### 1.4 写作前必读同类优秀论文\n\n- 每个章节动笔前，找 **2-3 篇目标会议近 1-2 年的高分论文**作为风格参考——同类问题或同类方法皆可\n- 重点学：\n  - Abstract 怎么用最少篇幅讲完故事\n  - Introduction 怎么从大问题切入到具体贡献\n  - Related Work 怎么 positioning 自己（不是 survey）\n  - Table/Figure 怎么压缩信息密度\n- **不是抄袭**，是学习同行的故事节奏和审美——不同子领域叙事节奏差异很大\n- 实操：Paper Agent 可以从 `literature_map.yaml` 中挑 `role=foundation` 或 `role=direct-competitor` 的论文，把 `{title, venue, year, abstract}` 附在 Worker 的 task_prompt 中作为概念参考；如需学习写作风格本身，让 Worker 通过 WebSearch 取得论文链接并 fetch 全文\n\n---\n\n## 二、Abstract 写作规范\n\n### 2.1 四个必答问题（150-250 words）\n\nAbstract 必须在很短的篇幅内清楚回答：\n1. **研究什么问题** — 1-2 句话交代背景和痛点\n2. **为什么重要** — 现有方法的不足（不用写太多）\n3. **本文提出什么** — 核心贡献的关键机制（简要，不堆砌细节）\n4. **结果说明什么** — 用具体实验结论支撑贡献\n\n### 2.2 禁忌\n\n- **禁止堆砌数据**：Abstract 不是 Results 的缩写。最多引用 2-3 个关键数字，要服务于 claim\n- **禁止过度宣传**：不说 \"revolutionary\"、\"groundbreaking\"\n- **禁止写成 Introduction 的缩写**：Abstract 是独立阅读单元\n- **禁止术语堆积**：如果审稿人几十秒内判断不出价值，就失败了\n- **缩写必须定义**：Abstract 中首次出现的缩写必须展开\n\n### 2.3 审稿人视角检验\n\n写完后用这个标准自检：审稿人能否在 30 秒内判断——\n- 问题是否重要？\n- 方法是否清楚？\n- 贡献是否可信？\n- 结果是否有说服力？\n\n---\n\n## 三、Introduction 写作规范\n\n### 3.1 完整叙事线（5 段结构）\n\nIntroduction 需要从一个清晰、重要的问题切入，逐步把读者带到核心贡献：\n\n1. **研究背景**（1-2 段）：大模型在某类能力/应用中已取得进展，但仍存在一个关键瓶颈\n2. **问题重要性**（1 段）：结合真实应用、已有方法局限或当前趋势，说明这不是小问题\n3. **现有方法不足**（1 段）：系统梳理为什么不够（依赖更大模型/更多数据/更复杂推理/更高成本，或只在特定 benchmark 有效）\n4. **本文核心想法**（1 段）：我们观察到什么、提出了什么方法、为什么能解决上述问题\n5. **贡献总结**（列表）：清楚列出方法贡献、实验贡献和分析贡献\n\n### 3.2 整体叙事结构\n\n```\n现有进展 → 关键缺口 → 本文洞察 → 方法概述 → 实验证据 → 贡献总结\n```\n\n### 3.3 禁忌\n\n- **禁止泛泛介绍**：不要开头就写 \"LLMs have shown remarkable capabilities...\"，要从具体问题切入\n- **禁止缺少过渡**：每段之间必须有逻辑连接\n- **禁止实验报告风格**：Introduction 不是 \"我们做了 X 实验得到了 Y 结果\" 的罗列\n- 每条 Contribution 必须有对应的 section/table 支撑\n- Contribution 列表中不能出现未在前文铺垫过的概念\n\n---\n\n## 四、Related Work 写作规范\n\n### 4.1 目标\n\n让审稿人相信：作者理解这个领域，知道自己的方法站在什么位置，清楚相对已有工作的真实增量在哪里。\n\n### 4.2 结构要求\n\n- 把已有工作分成几个清晰的主题（如模型训练、推理增强、工具调用、数据构建、评测等）\n- 每类简要说明：已有方法做了什么、解决了哪些问题、和本文的关系\n- 帮助读者理解：哪些是本文直接继承的，哪些方法不同但解决相似问题，哪些留下了本文要解决的缺口\n\n### 4.3 禁忌\n\n- **禁止论文堆砌**：不要写成 \"A proposed X. B proposed Y. C proposed Z.\" 的列表\n- **目标不是展示读了多少论文**，而是做归纳和比较\n- Contribution claim（\"To our knowledge, no prior work...\"）应放在 Introduction，不在 Related Work\n\n### 4.4 引用充足度\n\n- 引用必须覆盖：**直接相关方法、baseline 论文、评测 benchmark 论文、理论基础**\n- 任何一类缺失，审稿人会立刻看出来调研不充分\n- 引用密度参照目标会议近年录用论文的量级——明显偏少意味着 Related Work 调研不足\n\n---\n\n## 五、去 AI 写作风格（硬规则）\n\n以下模式是 AI 生成文本的典型标志，审稿人一眼能看出。**必须全部消除。**\n\n### 5.1 严禁破折号（最显著的 AI 痕迹）\n\n**这是 AI 写作最容易被识别的标志，审稿人最敏感。单列为最高优先级硬规则。**\n\n- 解释性 em-dash（—，U+2014）和 en-dash（–，U+2013）必须**全部删除**\n- 替代方式：用逗号、括号或拆分为两个句子\n- 强制检查命令：\n  ```bash\n  grep -n \"—\" paper/*.tex     # em-dash，每处人工判断是否必要\n  grep -n \" – \" paper/*.tex   # 解释性 en-dash，几乎都应删除\n  ```\n- 例外（允许保留）：表格分隔符、数字范围（`2020–2024`、`pp. 12–18`）、专有名词（`Newton–Raphson`）\n\n### 5.2 段落与句式\n\n- **禁止过度分段**：不需要每 2-3 句就换段，自然段 5-8 句是正常的\n- **减少关系从句**（which, that, who）：能用简洁直接的句子就不用从句\n- **禁止 -ing 短语结尾**：如 \"..., highlighting the applicability of...\" / \"..., demonstrating the effectiveness of...\"。这是 AI 写作最典型的标志之一\n- **禁止连续使用 \"Furthermore\" / \"Moreover\" / \"Additionally\"**：一段内最多用一个过渡词\n- **避免 \"It is worth noting that...\" / \"Notably, ...\"**：直接写要说的内容\n\n### 5.3 用词黑名单\n\n- 避免 \"delve into\" / \"leverage\" / \"utilize\" / \"facilitate\" — 用 \"explore\" / \"use\" / \"enable\"\n- 避免 \"a myriad of\" / \"a plethora of\" — 用 \"many\" / \"various\"\n- 避免 \"in the realm of\" / \"in the context of\" — 直接说\n- 避免 \"paradigm shift\" / \"novel\" 过度使用\n\n### 5.4 校对清单（4 步）\n\n1. 全文 proofread 语法和拼写\n2. 检查所有代词/指示词（this, that, it）是否指代明确\n3. **`grep \"—\"` + `grep \" – \"` 全文清零**（删除所有解释性 dash，§5.1）\n4. `grep \"ing,\"` 模式——大部分 -ing 分词短语需要改写\n\n---\n\n## 六、图表与视觉规范\n\n### 6.1 知识密度原则\n\n- **图表的信息必须紧凑**——不能为了美观而留大量白空间\n- 一张图如果只传达一个简单信息，要么缩小要么合并到其他图中\n- 能用 inline text 说清的不要占一整个 Figure\n\n### 6.2 表格规范\n\n- 表格和正文文字不能视觉重叠（检查 column 宽度和 alignment）\n- 小数精度全表统一\n- 最优加粗、次优下划线\n- Caption 必须自包含\n\n### 6.3 图片规范\n\n- 数值标签不能被线、bar、点遮住——用白色背景或偏移标签\n- 图中字体 >= 7pt（打印后仍可读）\n- 栏宽约束需匹配目标 venue 模板要求\n- 概念图/流程图要有信息量，不要画一个 trivial 的 pipeline 然后占半页\n\n### 6.4 空间效率\n\n- 顶会正文页数严格受限（具体页数查目标会议官网），每 cm² 都是宝贵资源\n- 优先使用 `\\subfigure` 合并相关图\n- Table 能 inline 就不要单独浮动\n- 图表占比与其对叙事的贡献成正比\n\n### 6.5 页面节奏\n\n好论文的页面有节奏感——text、equation、figure 交替出现，读者视觉不疲劳：\n\n- **text-equation-figure 三重奏**：重要概念用\"文字解释 → 公式定义 → 图表直觉\"三步展开\n- 连续 2 页纯文本（无 figure/equation/table）→ 穿插一个 display item 打破节奏\n- 连续 2 个 figure 页（整页被 float 占满）→ 至少隔 1 段文字再放下一个 float\n- Section heading 不孤悬页底（heading 后至少跟 2 行正文，否则强制分页）\n- 最后一页内容 ≥ 2/3 页面高度（太空显得凑数，太满放不下 references）\n\n### 6.6 表格 LaTeX 规范\n\n**Booktabs 标准（硬规则）**：\n- 用 `\\toprule` / `\\midrule` / `\\bottomrule`，禁止 `\\hline`，禁止竖线 `|`\n- 用 `\\centering` 不用 `\\begin{center}`（后者多加垂直空白）\n- 最优结果加粗（`\\textbf{}`），次优下划线（`\\underline{}`）+ 方向箭头 `↑`/`↓` 标明指标方向\n- 小数精度全表统一（如全部保留 1 位或 2 位）\n- Caption 在表格上方（`\\caption` 在 `\\begin{tabular}` 之前）\n\n**宽度决策树**：\n- ≤5 列 → 单栏宽度（`\\columnwidth`）\n- 6–8 列 → 全文宽度（`\\textwidth`，双栏模板用 `table*`）\n- >8 列 → 拆成两张表或移入 appendix\n\n---\n\n## 七、投稿前通用准备\n\n### 7.1 模板必须官方（硬规则）\n\n- LaTeX 模板**必须从目标会议官网下载**（CallForPapers / Author Instructions 页面）\n- **不要用**：\n  - 网上他人转载的复制版本（可能被改过 margin / 行号设置）\n  - 上一年的模板（参数可能变：字体、margin、行号、anonymous 选项等）\n  - 组里学长留的旧版本（来源不明、无法追溯）\n- 风险：\n  - 样式不符合 → desk reject\n  - margin 不对 → 被审稿人质疑诚信\n  - reference 格式错 → 被认为不规范\n- 流程：先查会议官网 templates → 找不到再 `WebSearch \"{venue} {year} latex template official\"` → 仍找不到汇报 Director（不要用就近年版本顶替）\n- `\\documentclass[]{}` 选项必须与目标 venue + 阶段（review / camera-ready）一致\n\n### 7.2 投稿要求逐项核对\n\n- 目标会议如要求 Checklist / Ethical Statement / Broader Impact Statement / Reproducibility Statement，必须**认真填每一项**，不要全写 N/A\n- 页数限制：查目标会议官网\n- 匿名化要求：review 版 vs camera-ready 版本设置不同\n- 行号开启（review 版常需）\n- 字体大小、margin 不得修改 sty 文件\n\n### 7.3 最终修改 10 项检查流程\n\n提交前必须逐一检查：\n1. 所有最新实验结果已全部录入\n2. 根据实验结果将叙事调至最优状态\n3. 附录所有内容在正文中被引用且融入叙事\n4. 无数据堆砌（每个数字服务于叙事推进）\n5. Abstract 和 Introduction 具有吸引力（不是实验报告风格）\n6. 全文主线统一（idea 变化后各章节逻辑一致）\n7. 所有专有名词有定义\n8. 无 AI 风格标志（dash、-ing phrases、过度分段）\n9. 图表知识密度高、无视觉遮挡\n10. Reference 覆盖直接相关方法、baseline、benchmark、理论基础\n",
      "numLines": 276,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 276
    }
  },

20	# 论文写作质量指南（NeurIPS 实战经验）
21	
22	> 来源：2026 年 NeurIPS 投稿期间，从 20+ 项目的人工审查反馈中提炼。
23	> 每条规则都源自真实的 bug / 退回 / 审稿质疑。
24	
25	---
26	
27	## 一、数据诚信与溯源（最高优先级）
28	
29	### 1.1 数字必须有源头
30	
31	- 论文中**每一个数字**必须可追溯到 registry 实验 ID 或原始结果文件
32	- 禁止凭记忆写数字——必须 `cat result.json | head` + `ls -la` 附上文件证据
33	- 计算值（均值、标准差、DID、delta）必须独立重算，不信 Agent 第一手汇报
34	- `paper_provenance.yaml` 覆盖率必须 100%，改数字必须同步更新 provenance
35	
36	> **教训（D149 事件）**：Agent 曾伪造完整的 metrics 数据包括 p-value，事后被 Reviewer 独立 parse JSON 抓出。从此所有关键数据必须 Reviewer 独立验证到字段级别。
37	
38	### 1.2 不同来源数据不能混用
39	
40	- 配对数据必须同 seed、同 c 值、同 N、同 evaluation protocol
41	- 两张表如果 protocol 不同（如 0-shot vs 8-shot），论文必须明确说明
42	- 跨 config 比较 delta 会引入系统误差——必须补跑同 config baseline 后才能比较
43	- 非等量对比必须显式标注（如 "at unmatched volumes"）
44	
45	> **教训（D027 事件）**：不同 config 文件的 baseline 不同，跨 config 比较 delta 值引入了 artifact。
46	
47	### 1.3 结论撤回后不可复活
48	
49	- 被 Director shelved 的数据（如因截断混淆），禁止重新引入论文
50	- `paper_provenance.yaml` 中 `verified: false` 的 claim，不得恢复
51	- 术语修正后不可回退（如 "same norm" → "same per-layer standard deviation"）
52	
53	---
54	
55	## 二、Claim 强度与数据匹配
56	
57	### 2.1 Claim 强度规则
58	
59	| 数据支撑 | 允许的最强表述 |
60	|----------|--------------|
61	| 单 seed, 单数据集 | "preliminary observation", "initial evidence" |
62	| 单 seed, 多数据集 | "suggestive pattern", "indicative trend" |
63	| 多 seed, 单数据集 | 报告均值±标准差，可写 "we find" |
64	| 多 seed, 多数据集 | 可用 "robust", "consistent" |
65	| 统计检验显著 | 可用 "significant", 附 p-value |
66	| 单 seed + 外推到其他条件 | 必须加限定，不能声称 "scale-invariant" 等 |
67	
68	### 2.2 禁止的过强表述
69	
70	- "remarkably stable" → 改为 "relatively stable" 或给出量化波动范围
71	- "confirmed" → 仅 "suggestive" 级别数据不能用
72	- "scale-invariant" → single-seed 验证不够
73	- "We attribute X to Y" → 如果是假说，必须改为 "We hypothesize" + "requiring causal verification"
74	- "roughly twice" → 实际比值必须在 1.8x-2.2x 才能用，否则改为更准确的近似
75	- "domain-agnostic" → 改为 "robust across N domains tested"
76	
77	### 2.3 Claim 降级机制
78	
79	当证据不足以支撑独立 claim 时，应降级为 discussion 中的定性讨论，而非删除。具体做法：
80	- 独立 contribution → Discussion 段落
81	- 硬 claim → 加 caveat 的软 claim
82	- 正文数字 → 移入 Appendix
83	
84	---
85	
86	## 三、叙事与 Storytelling
87	
88	### 3.1 结构原则
89	
90	- 核心 paradox/发现之间必须有**过渡句**桥接，不能让读者在关键处断线
91	- Introduction 每条 Contribution 必须有对应的 section/table 支撑
92	- Discussion 不应重复 Results 的具体数字——引用表格编号
93	- 同一解释不要在多处重复推导——主文写一次 + 指向 Appendix
94	- Limitations 段不要写成一个 17 行大段——拆分为 2-3 个子段按主题分组
95	
96	### 3.2 冷引用（Cold Reference）
97	
98	- 任何首次出现的概念必须有上文铺垫
99	- Introduction 贡献列表中不能突然出现未被提及的模型/方法名
100	
101	### 3.3 不要用旧叙事套新数字
102	
103	新 baseline 出来后若 delta 大幅变化，Section 的叙事逻辑必须重写，不是简单替换数字。
104	
105	### 3.4 Contingency Plan
106	
107	关键实验结果不确定时需预写两套叙事（如 "若结果 A → 叙事 A"、"若结果 B → 叙事 B"），实验完成后选择正确版本。提交前必须确认只留了一套。
108	
109	### 3.5 不要基于中间值做叙事决策
110	
111	70% 进度时的估计往往不够稳定（尤其 GSM8K 等难度分布不均匀的 benchmark），必须等完整数据再定稿叙事。
112	
113	---
114	
115	## 四、术语与一致性
116	
117	### 4.1 术语一致性
118	
119	- 全文必须统一使用正式定义的术语，不得出现同义词漂移
120	- 术语定义后，每次使用必须完全一致（不要有时 "perturbation sensitivity" 有时 "perturbation impact"）
121	
122	### 4.2 统计量一致性
123	
124	- 两个不同的 kappa/p-value 每次出现时必须标注是哪个
125	- 同一个 metric 在不同位置的数字必须完全一致
126	- 修改任何数字后必须全文 grep 确认所有引用处同步
127	
128	### 4.3 p-value 格式
129	
130	- 无前导零、3 位小数：`p = .003`（不是 `p = 0.003`）
131	- 当非劣效性 p<0.001 实际由正向效应驱动时，必须加括注说明
132	
133	### 4.4 例子与定义必须一致
134	
135	Taxonomy 中的定义和对应示例不能自相矛盾。如果定义说 "no new content"，示例中就不能包含新信息。
136	
137	---
138	
139	## 五、投稿前检查清单（Pre-Submission Checklist）
140	
141	### 5.1 占位符扫描（硬门禁）
142	
143	提交前必须 grep 扫描，以下模式要求**零匹配**：
144	```
145	XX.XX, PLACEHOLDER, [If , TODO, TBD, \placeholder{
146	```
147	所有条件占位段落（`[If ...]`）必须替换为最终文本。
148	
149	### 5.2 编译检查
150	
151	- [ ] 编译零 error
152	- [ ] 无 `??` 未解析引用
153	- [ ] 无 > 1pt 的 overfull hbox
154	- [ ] 正文 ≤ 9 页（NeurIPS desk reject 风险）
155	- [ ] 使用当年 style file（`neurips_2026.sty`）
156	- [ ] Review 版启用行号
157	- [ ] 匿名审稿版无作者姓名、无致谢
158	
159	### 5.3 数据完整性
160	
161	- [ ] 论文所有数字与 registry/provenance 交叉验证通过
162	- [ ] 计算值（均值、标准差、DID、delta）独立重算确认
163	- [ ] 所有 evaluation protocol 差异已在论文中说明
164	- [ ] 多 seed 结果报告均值和标准差
165	- [ ] 单 seed 数据在 Limitations 中声明
166	
167	### 5.4 图表检查
168	
169	- [ ] 所有 figure PDF 存在且路径匹配
170	- [ ] 图表不超栏宽（单栏 5.5in / 全宽 6.5in）
171	- [ ] 图中字体 ≥ 7pt
172	- [ ] 小数精度全表统一
173	- [ ] 最优结果加粗、次优下划线
174	- [ ] 表格标题（caption）自包含
175	- [ ] Forest plot 等可视化无 dummy 值（CI_LO=-99 等）
176	
177	### 5.5 引用检查
178	
179	- [ ] 引用覆盖：**直接相关方法、baseline 论文、评测 benchmark 论文、理论基础**（任一缺失说明调研不充分）
180	- [ ] 引用密度与目标会议近年录用论文同量级
181	- [ ] 引用键与 bib 条目 1:1 匹配
182	- [ ] 无孤儿 bib 条目
183	- [ ] 无幻觉引用（所有被引论文实际存在）
184	- [ ] 近年论文的年份/会议准确
185	- [ ] 审稿人要求的引用全部补齐
186	
187	### 5.6 匿名化检查
188	
189	- [ ] 源代码无硬编码绝对路径（`/Users/...`, `/root/...`）
190	- [ ] 配置文件无服务器路径
191	- [ ] Git history 已清理 author 信息（用 fresh repo）
192	- [ ] 项目管理文件不得提交（AGENTS.md, memory.md, registry.yaml, task_context/ 等）
193	- [ ] API Keys 通过环境变量，无泄露
194	
195	### 5.7 会议 Checklist 填写（如目标会议要求）
196	
197	- [ ] Claims: 摘要/Introduction 准确反映实验范围
198	- [ ] Limitations: 正文 Limitations 段 + Appendix 补充
199	- [ ] Experiments: 实验设置完整（exp_id, seed, 超参, 评估 protocol）
200	- [ ] Reproducibility: 数据集公开、代码可用
201	- [ ] Statistical Significance: 多 seed 均值+标准差、置信区间
202	- [ ] Compute: GPU 型号、训练时间、总 GPU-hours
203	- [ ] Licenses: 数据集/模型引用完整
204	- [ ] AI Assistants: 如实声明使用情况
205	- [ ] 每项认真回答（不要全写 N/A）
206	
207	### 5.8 叙事完整性
208	
209	- [ ] Conclusion 包含所有关键限定符（如 "under greedy decoding"）
210	- [ ] Abstract 与正文一致（不能一个写 "single seed" 另一个已是 "10 seeds"）
211	- [ ] 无 Appendix Version A/B 两套叙事残留
212	- [ ] 无 LaTeX 注释与正文混淆
213	- [ ] Contribution claim 在 Introduction 中（不在 Related Work 中）
214	- [ ] 所有专有名词/缩写首次出现时有定义（Abstract 内缩写也必须展开）
215	- [ ] Introduction 与 Method/Results 讲同一条主线（idea 变化后全文逻辑一致）
216	
217	### 5.9 去 AI 风格检查
218	
219	- [ ] **`grep "—" paper/*.tex` 全文清零**（解释性 em-dash 必须全删，最显著的 AI 痕迹）
220	- [ ] **`grep " – " paper/*.tex` 全文清零**（解释性 en-dash 必须全删）
221	- [ ] 无 "-ing," 分词短语结尾（如 "..., demonstrating the effectiveness of..."）
222	- [ ] 无连续 "Furthermore" / "Moreover" / "Additionally"（同段最多 1 个）
223	- [ ] 无 "It is worth noting that..." / "Notably, ..."
224	- [ ] 无 "delve into" / "leverage" / "utilize" / "a myriad of" / "a plethora of"
225	- [ ] 自然段 5-8 句是正常的，不要每 2-3 句就换段
226	
227	### 5.10 模板与同类论文核对
228	
229	- [ ] LaTeX 模板从目标会议官网下载（不是他人转载、不是上一年）
230	- [ ] `\documentclass[]{}` 选项匹配 review / camera-ready 阶段
231	- [ ] 写作前已参考 2-3 篇同领域近 1-2 年优秀论文，叙事节奏对齐
232	
233	---
234	
235	## 六、Reviewer 审查标准
236	
237	### 6.1 CRITICAL 级别（必须修复才能提交）
238	
239	- 论文数字与实验数据不一致
240	- Abstract 与正文矛盾
241	- Evaluation protocol 混淆（如 0-shot 写成 8-shot）
242	- 未经验证的数据写入论文
243	- 占位符残留
244	
245	### 6.2 WARNING 级别（应当修复）
246	
247	- Stale claims（更新了某处数字但忘了同步其他位置）
248	- 过强措辞不匹配数据支撑
249	- 隐式全因子设计误导（如写 "3×3×3=27" 但实际只有 15 格）
250	- 评估指标只描述了部分 domain
251	- 旧版数据引用
252	
253	### 6.3 数据一致性审查流程
254	
255	1. 从 registry 提取所有实验 metrics
256	2. 对照论文中每一个内联数字（到行号级别）
257	3. 独立重算所有衍生值
258	4. 不一致处标注为 CRITICAL
259	5. 近似表述检查实际比值是否在允许范围内
260	
261	---
262	
263	## 七、常见反模式速查表
264	
265	| 反模式 | 症状 | 修复方式 |
266	|--------|------|----------|
267	| Stale claim | 论文某处还引用旧数字 | 改数字后全文 grep 同步 |
268	| Protocol 混淆 | 不同表格用了不同 evaluation 但没说明 | 每张表注明 protocol |
269	| Agent 误报 | 实验结果缺乏文件证据 | 要求 `cat result.json` 截图 |
270	| 混用数据算比值 | 跨 seed/条件/N 的数据混合比较 | 只用严格配对的数据 |
271	| 叙事超越数据 | "confirmed" 但实际只是 suggestive | 匹配 claim 强度到 §2.1 |
272	| 冷引用 | 首次出现的概念无铺垫 | 先建立框架再引入 |
273	| 定义-例子矛盾 | Taxonomy 定义和示例冲突 | 审查所有定义与其示例 |
274	| Placeholder 残留 | XX.XX, TODO, [If...] 未清理 | 提交前 grep 硬门禁 |
275	| Path 泄露 | 代码/config 含服务器绝对路径 | 改为相对路径 |
276	| 重复推导 | 同一解释在 3 处各自推导一遍 | 主文一次 + 引用 Appendix |
277	| Discussion 重复 Results | Discussion 大量复制 Results 数字 | 改为引用表格编号 |
278	| 单 seed overclaim | 单 seed 数据声称 scale-invariant | 加 preliminary/hedging 限定 |
279	| Registry 不同步 | Registry 面值与实际状态不一致 | 以 SSH 实测为准 |
280	| vLLM 静默失败 | max_model_len 太小导致 100% 400 错误 | 预检必须发真实 prompt 验证 |
281	
282	---
283	
284	## 八、写论文前加载的 Skills
285	
286	写论文或做最终审查时，Director/Agent 应按需加载以下 skills：
287	- `latex-paper` — LaTeX 编译与格式
288	- `reference-audit` — 引用真实性审计
289	- `academic-writer` — ML 学术写作规范
290	- `paper-quality-lessons`（本 skill）— 实战经验教训
291	
292	---
293	
294	## 九、附录：投稿经验时间线教训
295	
296	1. **论文数字锁定时机**：最终编译前 24h 锁定所有数字，之后只允许格式修改
297	2. **GPU 不要空闲**：审稿期间如果 Agent 空闲，应该跑 ablation/扩展实验作为 rebuttal 储备
298	3. **匿名化提前做**：不要等到最后一天才检查路径泄露，fresh repo 需要时间清理
299	4. **9 页零余量策略**：NeurIPS 正文 9 页是硬限，每次新增内容必须等量压缩并编译验证
300	5. **Contingency 叙事提前写**：关键实验完成前就写好两版叙事，避免最后一刻仓促改稿
301	
````

</details>

实际由正向效应驱动时，必须加括注说明\n\n### 4.4 例子与定义必须一致\n\nTaxonomy 中的定义和对应示例不能自相矛盾。如果定义说 \"no new content\"，示例中就不能包含新信息。\n\n---\n\n## 五、投稿前检查清单（Pre-Submission Checklist）\n\n### 5.1 占位符扫描（硬门禁）\n\n提交前必须 grep 扫描，以下模式要求**零匹配**：\n```\nXX.XX, PLACEHOLDER, [If , TODO, TBD, \\placeholder{\n```\n所有条件占位段落（`[If ...]`）必须替换为最终文本。\n\n### 5.2 编译检查\n\n- [ ] 编译零 error\n- [ ] 无 `??` 未解析引用\n- [ ] 无 > 1pt 的 overfull hbox\n- [ ] 正文 ≤ 9 页（NeurIPS desk reject 风险）\n- [ ] 使用当年 style file（`neurips_2026.sty`）\n- [ ] Review 版启用行号\n- [ ] 匿名审稿版无作者姓名、无致谢\n\n### 5.3 数据完整性\n\n- [ ] 论文所有数字与 registry/provenance 交叉验证通过\n- [ ] 计算值（均值、标准差、DID、delta）独立重算确认\n- [ ] 所有 evaluation protocol 差异已在论文中说明\n- [ ] 多 seed 结果报告均值和标准差\n- [ ] 单 seed 数据在 Limitations 中声明\n\n### 5.4 图表检查\n\n- [ ] 所有 figure PDF 存在且路径匹配\n- [ ] 图表不超栏宽（单栏 5.5in / 全宽 6.5in）\n- [ ] 图中字体 ≥ 7pt\n- [ ] 小数精度全表统一\n- [ ] 最优结果加粗、次优下划线\n- [ ] 表格标题（caption）自包含\n- [ ] Forest plot 等可视化无 dummy 值（CI_LO=-99 等）\n\n### 5.5 引用检查\n\n- [ ] 引用覆盖：**直接相关方法、baseline 论文、评测 benchmark 论文、理论基础**（任一缺失说明调研不充分）\n- [ ] 引用密度与目标会议近年录用论文同量级\n- [ ] 引用键与 bib 条目 1:1 匹配\n- [ ] 无孤儿 bib 条目\n- [ ] 无幻觉引用（所有被引论文实际存在）\n- [ ] 近年论文的年份/会议准确\n- [ ] 审稿人要求的引用全部补齐\n\n### 5.6 匿名化检查\n\n- [ ] 源代码无硬编码绝对路径（`/Users/...`, `/root/...`）\n- [ ] 配置文件无服务器路径\n- [ ] Git history 已清理 author 信息（用 fresh repo）\n- [ ] 项目管理文件不得提交（AGENTS.md, memory.md, registry.yaml, task_context/ 等）\n- [ ] API Keys 通过环境变量，无泄露\n\n### 5.7 会议 Checklist 填写（如目标会议要求）\n\n- [ ] Claims: 摘要/Introduction 准确反映实验范围\n- [ ] Limitations: 正文 Limitations 段 + Appendix 补充\n- [ ] Experiments: 实验设置完整（exp_id, seed, 超参, 评估 protocol）\n- [ ] Reproducibility: 数据集公开、代码可用\n- [ ] Statistical Significance: 多 seed 均值+标准差、置信区间\n- [ ] Compute: GPU 型号、训练时间、总 GPU-hours\n- [ ] Licenses: 数据集/模型引用完整\n- [ ] AI Assistants: 如实声明使用情况\n- [ ] 每项认真回答（不要全写 N/A）\n\n### 5.8 叙事完整性\n\n- [ ] Conclusion 包含所有关键限定符（如 \"under greedy decoding\"）\n- [ ] Abstract 与正文一致（不能一个写 \"single seed\" 另一个已是 \"10 seeds\"）\n- [ ] 无 Appendix Version A/B 两套叙事残留\n- [ ] 无 LaTeX 注释与正文混淆\n- [ ] Contribution claim 在 Introduction 中（不在 Related Work 中）\n- [ ] 所有专有名词/缩写首次出现时有定义（Abstract 内缩写也必须展开）\n- [ ] Introduction 与 Method/Results 讲同一条主线（idea 变化后全文逻辑一致）\n\n### 5.9 去 AI 风格检查\n\n- [ ] **`grep \"—\" paper/*.tex` 全文清零**（解释性 em-dash 必须全删，最显著的 AI 痕迹）\n- [ ] **`grep \" – \" paper/*.tex` 全文清零**（解释性 en-dash 必须全删）\n- [ ] 无 \"-ing,\" 分词短语结尾（如 \"..., demonstrating the effectiveness of...\"）\n- [ ] 无连续 \"Furthermore\" / \"Moreover\" / \"Additionally\"（同段最多 1 个）\n- [ ] 无 \"It is worth noting that...\" / \"Notably, ...\"\n- [ ] 无 \"delve into\" / \"leverage\" / \"utilize\" / \"a myriad of\" / \"a plethora of\"\n- [ ] 自然段 5-8 句是正常的，不要每 2-3 句就换段\n\n### 5.10 模板与同类论文核对\n\n- [ ] LaTeX 模板从目标会议官网下载（不是他人转载、不是上一年）\n- [ ] `\\documentclass[]{}` 选项匹配 review / camera-ready 阶段\n- [ ] 写作前已参考 2-3 篇同领域近 1-2 年优秀论文，叙事节奏对齐\n\n---\n\n## 六、Reviewer 审查标准\n\n### 6.1 CRITICAL 级别（必须修复才能提交）\n\n- 论文数字与实验数据不一致\n- Abstract 与正文矛盾\n- Evaluation protocol 混淆（如 0-shot 写成 8-shot）\n- 未经验证的数据写入论文\n- 占位符残留\n\n### 6.2 WARNING 级别（应当修复）\n\n- Stale claims（更新了某处数字但忘了同步其他位置）\n- 过强措辞不匹配数据支撑\n- 隐式全因子设计误导（如写 \"3×3×3=27\" 但实际只有 15 格）\n- 评估指标只描述了部分 domain\n- 旧版数据引用\n\n### 6.3 数据一致性审查流程\n\n1. 从 registry 提取所有实验 metrics\n2. 对照论文中每一个内联数字（到行号级别）\n3. 独立重算所有衍生值\n4. 不一致处标注为 CRITICAL\n5. 近似表述检查实际比值是否在允许范围内\n\n---\n\n## 七、常见反模式速查表\n\n| 反模式 | 症状 | 修复方式 |\n|--------|------|----------|\n| Stale claim | 论文某处还引用旧数字 | 改数字后全文 grep 同步 |\n| Protocol 混淆 | 不同表格用了不同 evaluation 但没说明 | 每张表注明 protocol |\n| Agent 误报 | 实验结果缺乏文件证据 | 要求 `cat result.json` 截图 |\n| 混用数据算比值 | 跨 seed/条件/N 的数据混合比较 | 只用严格配对的数据 |\n| 叙事超越数据 | \"confirmed\" 但实际只是 suggestive | 匹配 claim 强度到 §2.1 |\n| 冷引用 | 首次出现的概念无铺垫 | 先建立框架再引入 |\n| 定义-例子矛盾 | Taxonomy 定义和示例冲突 | 审查所有定义与其示例 |\n| Placeholder 残留 | XX.XX, TODO, [If...] 未清理 | 提交前 grep 硬门禁 |\n| Path 泄露 | 代码/config 含服务器绝对路径 | 改为相对路径 |\n| 重复推导 | 同一解释在 3 处各自推导一遍 | 主文一次 + 引用 Appendix |\n| Discussion 重复 Results | Discussion 大量复制 Results 数字 | 改为引用表格编号 |\n| 单 seed overclaim | 单 seed 数据声称 scale-invariant | 加 preliminary/hedging 限定 |\n| Registry 不同步 | Registry 面值与实际状态不一致 | 以 SSH 实测为准 |\n| vLLM 静默失败 | max_model_len 太小导致 100% 400 错误 | 预检必须发真实 prompt 验证 |\n\n---\n\n## 八、写论文前加载的 Skills\n\n写论文或做最终审查时，Director/Agent 应按需加载以下 skills：\n- `latex-paper` — LaTeX 编译与格式\n- `reference-audit` — 引用真实性审计\n- `academic-writer` — ML 学术写作规范\n- `paper-quality-lessons`（本 skill）— 实战经验教训\n\n---\n\n## 九、附录：投稿经验时间线教训\n\n1. **论文数字锁定时机**：最终编译前 24h 锁定所有数字，之后只允许格式修改\n2. **GPU 不要空闲**：审稿期间如果 Agent 空闲，应该跑 ablation/扩展实验作为 rebuttal 储备\n3. **匿名化提前做**：不要等到最后一天才检查路径泄露，fresh repo 需要时间清理\n4. **9 页零余量策略**：NeurIPS 正文 9 页是硬限，每次新增内容必须等量压缩并编译验证\n5. **Contingency 叙事提前写**：关键实验完成前就写好两版叙事，避免最后一刻仓促改稿\n",

1	\section{Discussion and Conclusion}\label{sec:discussion}
2	
3	\paragraph{Granger precedence is not causation.}
4	Granger causality tests predictive improvement, not true causation~\cite{granger1969investigating,shojaie2022granger}: an unobserved process could independently drive both token entropy and diversity metrics.
5	Our central claim is scoped accordingly---``token entropy Granger-precedes diversity collapse''---and bivariate tests cannot exclude confounders; multivariate VAR models would be required to test for confounders directly.
6	
7	\paragraph{Practical utility paradox.}
8	The framework works best where it is least needed: at $\alpha{\geq}0.75$, PermG reliably detects canary precedence, but degradation is already severe; at $\alpha{\leq}0.45$, where early warning would be most valuable, detection remains null.
9	However, in realistic pipelines, contamination $\alpha$ is unknown and typically increases over time as web-scraped corpora accumulate machine-generated text.
10	A continuous monitoring dashboard would trigger when $\alpha$ crosses the detectability threshold, converting the static limitation into a temporal advantage.
11	The regime map itself is actionable: practitioners who know $\alpha{<}0.25$ should invest in data provenance rather than metric monitoring.
12	
13	\paragraph{Four-layer detection taxonomy.}
14	Our results suggest a layered taxonomy ordered by onset threshold:
15	(1)~distributional dimensionality (effective rank, onset $\alpha{\approx}0.10$);
16	(2)~distributional instability (entropy variance, suggestive at $\alpha{\approx}0.25$);
17	(3)~causal coupling (Granger-type, onset $\alpha_c{\approx}0.50$); and
18	(4)~dynamical stability (AC1, suggestive but not conclusive at $T{=}11$).
19	The 40-percentage-point gap between structural distortion (Layer~1) and causal evidence (Layer~3) constitutes the sharp detection onset.
20	Layers~1 and~3 are established; Layers~2 and~4 require longer generation protocols ($T{\geq}50$; \citealt{dakos2012methods}).
21	
22	\paragraph{Synchronous collapse at $\alpha{=}0.60$.}
23	The non-monotonic dip at $\alpha{=}0.60$ (PermG fwd below both $\alpha{=}0.50$ and $\alpha{=}0.75$) is consistent with the $S \times R$ prediction (\S\ref{sec:theory}): relaxation time matching drives $R \to 0$, so entropy and diversity respond within the same generation despite rising signal strength.
24	This creates a four-regime structure---blind, onset, synchronous dip, detection---rather than the binary boundary assumed by simpler models.
25	
26	\paragraph{Method recommendation.}
27	The primary value of the multi-method framework is the systematic comparison it enables.
28	By applying five methods with different inductive biases, the framework reveals why each succeeds or fails: naive cross-correlation inflates FPR via reverse-directional associations; differenced cross-correlation cannot separate forward from reverse; threshold onset lacks power at $T{=}11$; Toda--Yamamoto achieves moderate detection (3--4/8) but is sensitive to augmentation order.
29	This process of elimination converges on PermG as the recommended method: lowest null-control FPR among directionally-specific methods (11.1\%, 4/36 across 6 seeds; DiffXCorr achieves 2.8\% but cannot separate forward from reverse), consistent detection at $\alpha{\geq}0.50$, and clean directional separation (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse at $\alpha{=}1.0$ on WikiText).
30	
31	\paragraph{Conclusion.}
32	Lead-lag analysis in iterative training collapse is far more fragile than assumed: method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to 75\% null-control FPR, first-differencing exposes canary$\to$perplexity associations as entirely spurious, and regime dependence causes the same test to shift from significant to null.
33	PermG identifies token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse, validated across 15 model-seed combinations spanning six families (345M--6.9B) with detection in 13 cases (87\%).
34	Directional asymmetry is domain-dependent (unidirectional on WikiText, bidirectional on C4), and canary-based predictive cessation reduces peak perplexity by 58\%.
35	Extended discussion of cross-architecture generalization, domain-dependent directionality, token entropy versus perplexity, NLP-specific constraints, and future directions appears in Appendix~\ref{app:extended_discussion}.
36	
```

1	\begin{abstract}
2	Iterative training on model-generated text degrades distributional quality well before standard metrics flag collapse, yet detecting this degradation is difficult: the time series are short ($T{\leq}25$ generations), the signal grows sub-linearly with contamination fraction, and, as we show, the choice of statistical test alone can shift conclusions from universal detection to complete null.
3	We map the detectability landscape of this process on Pythia-410M across 13 contamination levels; cross-architecture evaluation at $\alpha{=}1.0$ supports forward detection in 11/15 conditions across five families (345M--6.9B), with four non-confirming conditions isolating distinct failure modes.
4	Permutation Granger causality (PermG) reveals a detection boundary that follows power-law scaling in observation length (leave-one-$T$-out CV: MAE${=}0.037$, 15/15 within CI) and four regimes, including a sharp onset where detection jumps from 0/8 to 6/8 within a 2-percentage-point contamination interval.
5	Five methods applied to identical data yield null-control false positive rates from 3\% to 92\%, exposing method sensitivity as a primary concern.
6	Entropy Granger-precedes diversity collapse by ${\geq}1$ generation (14/15 experiments), providing a canary whose triggered cessation substantially reduces peak perplexity in a proof-of-concept demonstration ($N{=}2$, $\alpha{=}1.0$).
7	\end{abstract}
8	
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01VvARUMQrVt4DgGV5thJz6E`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	When language models train iteratively on their own output, the training distribution progressively loses its tail modes, a phenomenon termed model collapse~\cite{shumailov2024curse,alemohammad2024mad}.
5	Lexical diversity narrows, calibration deteriorates, and each generation inherits the systematic biases of its predecessors.
6	As machine-generated text becomes prevalent on the web, such feedback loops pose a growing and often invisible risk to production training pipelines.
7	By the time standard quality metrics register degradation, the underlying distribution may have already shifted irreversibly.
8	This threat motivates the search for cheap-to-compute \emph{canary metrics}: leading indicators that signal distributional degradation before expensive downstream measures reveal the full extent of collapse.
9	The concept parallels early-warning signals for critical transitions in ecological systems~\cite{scheffer2009early,scheffer2012anticipating}, where proxy indicators allow intervention before a tipping point.
10	
11	Yet a fundamental question remains unanswered: \emph{at what contamination level does Granger-causal detection of collapse become possible?}
12	Whether a candidate canary genuinely \emph{precedes} downstream degradation or merely correlates with it under shared non-stationary trends depends on contamination regime and statistical method in ways that existing analyses leave unresolved.
13	Iterative training produces short, noisy time series (often fewer than 15 points per generation sequence), where detection power drops sharply and false positive rates can be high~\cite{boettiger2012limits}.
14	
15	We identify four deficiencies in existing lead-lag analyses of iterative training collapse.
16	(1)~No differencing tests rule out spurious regression from shared non-stationary trends~\cite{granger1974spurious}.
17	(2)~Conclusions rest on a single statistical method whose assumptions about stationarity and directionality are rarely examined.
18	(3)~No FDR correction guards against spurious findings when testing many metric pairs simultaneously.
19	(4)~No regime-dependent analysis is performed, despite the possibility that the same test yields opposite conclusions at different contamination levels.
20	
21	We discover a \emph{sharp detection onset} in canary-based monitoring of iterative collapse.
22	Using Pythia-410M~\cite{biderman2023pythia} across thirteen contamination levels ($\alpha \in \{0.00, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 0.30, 0.35, 0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60, 0.75, 1.00\}$) and up to four random seeds, we show that causal detectability undergoes a steep onset at per-generation mixing ratio $\alpha_c{=}0.476$ at $T{=}10$ generations (sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\Delta\alpha{\approx}0.02$): permutation Granger causality (PermG) jumps from 0/8 FDR-significant metric pairs at $\alpha{\leq}0.45$ to 6/8 at $\alpha{=}0.50$.
23	A signal-times-resolution ($S \times R$) framework explains this onset and the non-monotonic dose-response through competing signal strength and temporal resolution.
24	A multi-method consensus framework (MMCT) comparing five statistical methods reveals that method choice alone can shift conclusions on the same data from universal significance to complete null.
25	Cross-architecture validation across 15 model-seed combinations spanning six families (345M--6.9B) confirms PermG detection (${\geq}6/8$) in 13 cases (87\%).
26	Granger precedence in our framework establishes temporal predictive improvement, not true causality~\cite{shojaie2022granger}; our canary claim is scoped to diversity metrics (distinct-$n$~\cite{li2016diversity}), not model collapse broadly.
27	
28	Our contributions are:
29	\begin{enumerate}
30	    \item \textbf{Sharp detection onset} (\S\ref{sec:regime}).
31	    At $T{=}10$, PermG jumps from 0/8 FDR-significant pairs through $\alpha{=}0.45$ to 6/8 at $\alpha{=}0.50$ ($\alpha_c{=}0.476$, sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\Delta\alpha{\approx}0.02$), with a four-regime structure: \emph{blind}, \emph{onset}, \emph{synchronous dip}, and \emph{detection}.
32	    At $\alpha{=}0.10$, a canary blind spot emerges: diversity has already collapsed but canary metrics remain indistinguishable from the null.
33	    Cross-architecture validation across 15 model-seed combinations confirms detection in 13 cases (87\%).
34	
35	    \item \textbf{Signal-times-resolution theory} (\S\ref{sec:theory}).
36	    A power interpretation formalizes the onset as $\mathrm{Power}(\alpha, T) = \Phi(S \cdot R \cdot \sqrt{T}/\sigma)$, generating three testable predictions for $T{=}20$; sub-threshold PermG $p$-values at $\alpha{=}0.45$ ($p{=}0.059$--$0.091$) are consistent with proximity to the power boundary.
37	
38	    \item \textbf{Multi-method diagnostic toolkit} (\S\ref{sec:method}).
39	    The MMCT exposes three pitfalls: method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to 75\% ($\kappa{=}0.185$), first-differencing eliminates canary$\to$perplexity associations entirely while preserving all canary$\to$diversity leads (24/24$\to$24/24), and regime dependence causes the same test to shift from 5--6/8 FDR-significant pairs per seed at $\alpha{\geq}0.75$ to 0/8 at $\alpha{\leq}0.45$.
40	    PermG identifies token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse).
41	
42	    \item \textbf{Canary-based early detection} (\S\ref{sec:intervention}).
43	    Predictive cessation reduces peak perplexity by 58\% (bootstrap 95\% CI: 29--64\%) and fires one generation earlier than reactive cessation.
44	    A four-layer detection taxonomy provides regime-appropriate diagnostics from $\alpha{\geq}0.10$ to $\alpha{\geq}0.50$.
45	\end{enumerate}
46	
```

```text
1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\paragraph{Methodological pitfalls.}
5	\label{sec:pitfalls}
6	Lead-lag analysis in iterative model collapse is fragile along three axes, each documented with data from \S\ref{sec:regime}--\ref{sec:dose_response}.
7	\emph{Method sensitivity}: five detection methods applied to identical data ($\alpha{=}1.0$, 16 canary$\to$downstream pairs) yield significance rates from 100\% (naive cross-correlation) to 37.5\% (permutation Granger), with inter-method agreement at $\kappa{=}0.185$ and a spurious-signal rate ${\geq}62.5\%$.
8	\emph{Spurious regression}: first-differencing to remove shared trends~\cite{granger1974spurious} eliminates canary$\to$perplexity associations ($F{\approx}0$, $p{=}0.99$) while preserving canary$\to$diversity (24/24$\to$24/24), exposing an entire analysis channel as trend-driven rather than temporally precedent.
9	\emph{Regime dependence}: the same Granger test finds 0/8 pairs significant through $\alpha{=}0.45$ but 6/8 at $\alpha{=}0.50$, a categorical shift at a 5-percentage-point boundary that a single-$\alpha$ study would miss entirely.
10	These pitfalls motivate systematic method comparison rather than reliance on any single detection approach.
11	
12	\paragraph{Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT).}
13	\label{sec:mmct}
14	We apply the Multi-Method Consensus Test, a weighted consensus framework in the specification curve~\cite{simonsohn2020specification} and multi-method early-warning~\cite{scheffer2009early,bell2022modeling} traditions, adapted to the constraints of iterative training analysis: short series ($T{=}5$--$20$), non-stationarity by design, and heterogeneous method sensitivities.
15	MMCT is a consensus testing protocol, not a novel statistical method.
16	It compares five lead-lag detection methods through FPR-inverse weighted consensus, calibrated against null-control ($\alpha{=}0.0$) data where any detection is a false positive.
17	Each method $m_k$ receives weight $w_k = 1/\max(\hat{\text{FPR}}_k, \epsilon)$ with $\epsilon{=}0.01$; the weighted score $W(X \to Y) = \sum_k w_k d_k / \sum_k w_k$ yields formal $p$-values from the empirical null distribution (Algorithm~\ref{alg:mmct}; Appendix).
18	MMCT identifies permutation Granger (PermG) as the method with the lowest null-control FPR among directionally-specific methods (11.1\%, 4/36 across 6 seeds; DiffXCorr achieves 2.8\% but lacks directional separation) combined with consistent detection across contamination levels, and we recommend it as the primary detection method based on this systematic comparison.
19	
20	\paragraph{Permutation Granger causality.}
21	PermG tests whether past values of canary metric $X$ improve prediction of downstream metric $Y$ beyond $Y$'s own history; Granger precedence establishes temporal predictive improvement, not true causality~\cite{shojaie2022granger}.
22	We apply 5{,}000 permutations on first-differenced series with Benjamini--Hochberg FDR correction at $q{=}0.05$ across 8 validated canary$\to$downstream pairs (2 canaries $\times$ 4 downstream metrics).
23	Three properties distinguish PermG: a nonparametric null distribution robust to the short non-stationary series typical of iterative training~\cite{boettiger2012limits}, built-in directionality (forward and reverse tested separately), and a hyperparameter-free test statistic.
24	The remaining four methods each exhibit a specific limitation: naive cross-correlation has 75\% null-control FPR; differenced cross-correlation detects bidirectionally without directional separation; threshold onset is power-limited at $T{=}11$ under corrected $3\sigma$ calibration (0/8 significant in 8 of 12 cross-architecture conditions); Toda--Yamamoto~\cite{toda1995statistical} achieves moderate supplementary detection (3--4/8 at $\alpha{\geq}0.50$) with reduced power from degree-of-freedom costs of lag augmentation.
25	Per-method dose-response profiles, Shapley value analysis, and the full MMCT algorithm appear in the Appendix.
26	
27	\begin{figure*}[t]
28	\centering
29	\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{figures/fig_framework.pdf}
30	\caption{Analytical framework overview. (a)~Iterative contamination loop: each generation fine-tunes on an $\alpha$-fraction synthetic mixture, producing canary (entropy, ECE) and downstream (distinct-$n$, perplexity) time series. (b)~Multi-Method Consensus Test: five detection methods weighted by inverse null-control FPR converge to a $K{\geq}3$ consensus decision. (c)~Detection regimes in the $(\alpha, T)$ plane: the boundary $\alpha_c(T)$ separates blind and detection zones, with onset and sync-dip as transitional regions.}
31	\label{fig:framework}
32	\end{figure*}
33	
34	\subsection{Signal-Times-Resolution Framework}
35	\label{sec:theory}
36	
37	We apply standard statistical power scaling to PermG under the Signal$\times$Resolution (S$\times$R) framework.
38	Detectability of canary$\to$downstream temporal precedence depends on two competing factors:
39	\begin{equation}
40	    \text{Detectability}(\alpha) \;\propto\; S(\alpha) \times R(\alpha),
41	    \label{eq:detectability}
42	\end{equation}
43	where $S(\alpha)$ is signal strength (magnitude of distributional shift, monotonically increasing with $\alpha$) and $R(\alpha)$ is temporal resolution (the generational gap between canary and downstream responses).
44	$R(\alpha)$ is non-monotonic: at low $\alpha$, entropy and diversity respond in different generations ($R$ large); at $\alpha{\approx}0.60$, relaxation time matching compresses both responses into a single generation ($R \to 0$); at high $\alpha$, the severity of entropy collapse outpaces the diversity cascade, restoring the generational offset.
45	The product $S \times R$ predicts four regimes: \emph{blind} ($\alpha \leq 0.45$; $S$ below detection floor), \emph{onset} ($\alpha \approx 0.50$; $S$ crosses threshold while $R$ remains high), \emph{synchronous dip} ($\alpha \approx 0.60$; $R \to 0$ suppresses detection despite rising $S$), and \emph{detection} ($\alpha \geq 0.75$; $S$ dominates).
46	Full empirical confirmation appears in \S\ref{sec:regime} and \S\ref{sec:dose_response}.
47	
48	Under a permutation test framework, detection power scales as $\mathrm{Power}(\alpha, T) = \Phi\bigl(S \cdot R \cdot \sqrt{T} / \sigma_{\mathrm{noise}}\bigr)$, where $\Phi$ is the standard normal CDF and the $\sqrt{T}$ scaling follows standard power analysis.
49	The sharp detection onset at $\alpha_c$ (at $T{=}10$) is consistent with a power threshold: when $S \cdot R \cdot \sqrt{T}$ first exceeds the detection floor, power undergoes a steep onset from near-zero to near-one.
50	This generates three predictions for $T{=}20$: (i)~$\alpha_c$ left-shifts as increased $T$ raises power at all $\alpha$; (ii)~the transition smooths as $\sqrt{T}$ broadens the power curve; (iii)~different methods exhibit different $\alpha_c$ values due to method-specific power curves.
51	Bootstrap calibration ($N{=}10{,}000$) yields clean falsifiable tests at $\alpha{\geq}0.75$: predicted 7/8 (95\% CI: [6,8]); results below 6/8 would falsify $\sqrt{T}$ power scaling.
52	At $\alpha{=}0.45$, the point prediction is 0/8, but the bootstrap CI spans [0,7] reflecting $\alpha_c$ uncertainty---both detection and non-detection are informative for constraining the phase boundary location.
53	At $\alpha{=}0.50$, the prediction is inherently uncertain at the transition (CI: [0,7]).
54	Sub-threshold FDR $p{=}0.059$--$0.091$ at $\alpha{=}0.45$ is consistent with proximity to the power boundary.
55	
56	\paragraph{Calibration.}
57	We validate error rates with built-in experimental controls.
58	False positive rate: at $\alpha{=}0.0$ (null control, no synthetic contamination), PermG achieves the lowest FPR among directionally-specific methods: 11.1\% across 6 seeds (4/36 pairs; Clopper--Pearson 95\% CI $[3.1\%, 26.1\%]$); under identical BH-FDR correction, DiffXCorr achieves the lowest FPR (2.8\%) but lacks forward/reverse directional separation.
59	False negative rate: at $\alpha{=}1.0$, all 6 effect-size reference pairs (2 canaries $\times$ 3 diversity metrics, selected by criteria independent of the detection methods) are recovered at 0\% FNR across 4 seeds.
60	PermG matches consensus performance at all tested contamination levels, with cross-architecture validation confirming PermG forward significance ${\geq}6/8$ in 11 of 15 conditions (\S\ref{sec:generalization}).
61	Full calibration details, practical guidance tiers, and method-level breakdown appear in the Appendix.
62	
```

```text
1	\subsection{Sharp Detection Onset}
2	\label{sec:regime}
3	
4	Diversity collapse occurs at every contamination level: distinct-1 declines by $14.1\%$ at $\alpha{=}0.0$ (fine-tuning drift) and by $40.3{\pm}0.3\%$ at $\alpha{=}0.25$ (3-seed mean).
5	Despite this ubiquitous degradation, Granger-causal detectability is sharply regime-dependent.
6	Permutation Granger (PermG) tests find 0/8 canary$\to$downstream pairs FDR-significant from $\alpha{=}0.00$ through $\alpha{=}0.45$, then jump to 6/8 at $\alpha{=}0.50$ (3~fp32 seeds, consistent).
7	This transition is striking because cumulative synthetic fraction barely changes across the boundary: $C = 1{-}(1{-}\alpha)^{10}$ yields $99.75\%$ at $\alpha{=}0.45$ versus $99.90\%$ at $\alpha{=}0.50$, a difference of 0.15 percentage points, yet detection jumps from null to reliable.
8	Sigmoid regression across the 13-point $\alpha$ grid quantifies this steep onset: $\alpha_c = 0.476$ at $T{=}10$, steepness $k \geq 200$, transition width $\Delta\alpha \approx 0.02$, $R^2 = 0.969$ (versus power-law $R^2 = 0.808$, $\Delta\mathrm{AIC} = 18.2$; Figure~\ref{fig:phase_transition}).
9	At $\alpha{=}0.45$, PermG $p$-values reveal sub-threshold signal ($p_{\mathrm{fdr}} = 0.059$ for seed~43, $0.091$ for seed~42), consistent with the power-threshold interpretation of $\alpha_c$ at $T{=}10$ (\S\ref{sec:theory}): the onset reflects PermG's power curve crossing the detection floor, not the emergence of a new physical signal.
10	A specification curve analysis spanning 1{,}160 analytical specifications confirms that the sharp detection onset is specification-invariant.
11	
12	First-differencing scopes the canary claim precisely.
13	Canary$\to$diversity pairs retain unanimous FDR significance after first-differencing ($24/24 \to 24/24$ across 4~seeds at $\alpha{=}1.0$), while canary$\to$perplexity collapses entirely ($F \approx 0$, $p = 0.99$): the apparent perplexity association is a shared-trend artifact.
14	All canary claims are therefore scoped to diversity metrics only.
15	At $\alpha{=}1.0$, 24/24 forward-direction pairs reach FDR significance with 0/24 reverse (4~seeds); directional asymmetry is robust across all WikiText conditions.
16	The canary mechanism also exhibits a blind spot: at $\alpha{=}0.10$, distinct-1 has already dropped by $39.6\%$, yet token entropy is indistinguishable from the null ($-5.3\%$ versus $-4.6{\pm}0.4\%$ at $\alpha{=}0.0$).\footnote{Seed~43 at $\alpha{=}0.10$ shows 3/8 PermG FDR-significant pairs versus 0/8 for seed~42, consistent with stochastic variation at low signal rather than robust detection; no other below-threshold $\alpha$ level exhibits seed-dependent detection.}
17	Below $\alpha_c$ at $T{=}10$, diversity collapse proceeds without any detectable Granger-causal signal in the canary metrics.
18	
19	\begin{figure}[t]
20	\centering
21	\includegraphics[width=\linewidth]{figures/metric_trajectory.pdf}
22	\caption{Metric trajectories at $\alpha{=}1.0$ (Pythia-410M, 4~seeds). Token entropy drops at generation~1; distinct-1 declines from generations~2--3, with a consistent one-generation canary lead across all four seeds (shaded: inter-seed range). This pattern corresponds to 24/24 forward and 0/24 reverse FDR-significant pairs.}
23	\label{fig:metric_trajectory}
24	\end{figure}
25	
26	
27	\subsection{Dose-Response and the $S \times R$ Framework}
28	\label{sec:dose_response}
29	
30	The PermG dose-response profile (Figure~\ref{fig:regime_map}) reveals four detection regimes: \emph{blind} ($\alpha \leq 0.45$, 0/8 FDR-significant pairs), \emph{onset} ($\alpha \approx 0.50$, 6/8), \emph{synchronous dip} ($\alpha \approx 0.60$, 0/8 both seeds; dual-seed confirmed), and \emph{detection} ($\alpha{=}0.75$, 0--6/8 per seed, 2 of 3 seeds ${\geq}5/8$; $\alpha{=}1.0$, 6/8 across 4~seeds).
31	The non-monotonic dip at $\alpha{=}0.60$ is consistent with the $S \times R$ framework (\S\ref{sec:theory}): signal strength $S(\alpha)$ rises monotonically with contamination, but temporal resolution $R(\alpha)$ drops when relaxation time approaches the observation interval.
32	At $\alpha \approx 0.60$, this compression drives the canary-to-downstream lag toward zero, and the product $S \times R$ reaches a local minimum.
33	The synchronous zone is mechanistically distinct from the blind spot: in the blind zone, canary drift is indistinguishable from the null; in the synchronous zone, both canary and downstream metrics drift substantially but without temporal offset.
34	At $\alpha{=}0.55$, PermG returns 0/8 FDR-significant pairs despite marginal $p$-values ($p_{\mathrm{fdr}}{=}0.062$--$0.092$), consistent with power-limited non-detection between onset and synchronous dip; differenced cross-correlation detects 8/8 bidirectionally at this level, confirming that a lead-lag signal exists but falls below PermG's directional detection threshold.
35	
36	Method choice is itself a major source of analytic variability.
37	At $\alpha{=}1.0$, forward-direction significance rates range from 100\% (naive cross-correlation) to 37.5\% (PermG), with inter-method $\kappa = 0.185$ (near chance) and a spurious rate of at least 62.5\%.
38	PermG achieves the lowest null-control FPR among directionally-specific methods (11.1\%, 4/36 across 6 seeds; DiffXCorr achieves 2.8\% but lacks directional separation) with consistent detection at $\alpha \geq 0.50$ (Table~\ref{tab:method_comparison}); its nonparametric null distribution, built-in directionality, and hyperparameter-free design explain this advantage.
39	The $S \times R$ framework generates three testable predictions for $T{=}20$ via $\mathrm{Power}(\alpha, T) = \Phi(S \cdot R \cdot \sqrt{T}/\sigma)$: (i)~$\alpha_c$ at $T{=}10$ should left-shift, (ii)~the transition should smooth, and (iii)~different methods should exhibit different $\alpha_c$ values.
40	These predictions are under empirical verification.
41

68	
69	\subsection{Cross-Architecture Generalization}
70	\label{sec:generalization}
71	
72	We evaluate the sharp detection onset across 15 model-seed combinations spanning six families (Pythia, GPT-2, OPT, Qwen, SmolLM3, Padding-variant), scales from 345M to 6.9B parameters, and two training domains (WikiText-103, C4).
73	Table~\ref{tab:generalization} reports PermG forward detection at $\alpha{=}1.0$.
74	PermG forward significance reaches ${\geq}$6/8 in 11 of 15 conditions (73\%).
75	Non-confirming conditions span precision sensitivity (GPT-2 bf16: 0/8 vs fp32: 6/8), bidirectional confounding (GPT-2 s43: 6/8 both directions), seed dependence (Pythia-1.4B: s42 2/8, s43 6/8), and entropy decoupling (SmolLM3-3B: 3/8, entropy 0/4).
76	SmolLM3's token entropy exhibits non-monotonic oscillations across generations; this pattern violates the monotonic decline that Granger tests require.
77	The onset boundary $\alpha_c \approx 0.50$ at $T{=}10$ is architecture-independent: every family that achieves detection does so at or above this threshold, and none achieves reliable detection below it.
78	
79	Replacing WikiText-103 with C4 yields the strongest detection observed: Pythia-1B reaches PermG~fwd 8/8 (2~seeds), and Pythia-1.4B reaches 8/8 on C4 versus 2/8 on WikiText at the same scale.
80	At Pythia-6.9B (bf16), detection recovers to 7/8 on WikiText.
81	Dataset characteristics modulate detection power independently of model capacity.
82	Directional asymmetry also varies by domain: at $\alpha{=}0.50$ on C4, PermG reverse reaches 7/8, compared to 0/24 across all WikiText conditions.
83	This domain-dependent bidirectionality suggests that the unidirectional forward causation observed on WikiText may partly reflect domain-specific dynamics (\S\ref{sec:discussion}).
84

116	
117	
118	\subsection{Canary-Based Early Intervention}
119	\label{sec:intervention}
120	
121	We implement a predictive cessation protocol: training with synthetic data ($\alpha{=}1.0$) continues until token entropy drops ${>}15\%$ relative to generation~0, at which point $\alpha$ switches to $0.0$ (pure real data).
122	A reactive baseline triggers when distinct-1 drops ${>}10\%$.
123	The predictive trigger fires at generation~1 in both Pythia-410M and Pythia-1.4B (s42 fp32), one generation before the reactive baseline (generation~2).
124	Peak perplexity under predictive intervention is $56.99$ versus $157.09$ under reactive (s42 fp32), a 58\% reduction (bootstrap 95\% CI: 29--64\%).
125	Recovery occurs at generation~2 under predictive cessation versus generation~4 under reactive.
126	Both models converge to the $\alpha{=}0.0$ baseline by generation~10.
127	
128	The trigger generation, recovery timeline, and convergence behavior are identical across the $3.4\times$ scale gap from 410M to 1.4B, and the 15\% entropy threshold generalizes without recalibration.
129	These results position canary-based detection within a four-layer taxonomy of collapse monitoring: (1)~dimensionality collapse, detectable at $\alpha \geq 0.10$ via $n$-gram diversity decline; (2)~distributional variance, detectable at $\alpha \geq 0.25$ via calibration drift; (3)~causal coupling, detectable at $\alpha \geq 0.50$ via Granger precedence; and (4)~dynamical stability, assessable at $\alpha \geq 0.50$ via lag structure.
130	Each layer captures a distinct aspect of collapse, and lower layers activate at lower contamination thresholds.
131	The practical implication is that canary monitoring should combine multiple detection layers rather than rely on any single statistical test.
132	
```

```text
1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	Lead-lag analysis in iterative training collapse is far more fragile than the current literature assumes.
5	Method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to at least 62.5\%, first-differencing exposes canary$\to$perplexity associations as entirely spurious, and regime dependence causes the same Granger test to shift from 24/24 to 0/8 significant pairs across contamination levels.
6	These design considerations establish that single-method, single-regime analyses (the current default) cannot reliably distinguish genuine temporal precedence from statistical artifacts.
7	
8	Our systematic five-method comparison through the Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT) identifies permutation Granger causality as the recommended detection method: it combines the lowest null-control FPR among directionally-specific methods (11.1\%, 4/36 across 6 seeds; DiffXCorr achieves 2.8\% but lacks directional separation) with high sensitivity, exhibiting a steep dose-response onset from 0/8 (blind spot, $\alpha{\leq}0.45$) to 6/8 ($\alpha{=}0.50$; sigmoid $k{=}15.7$, ${\approx}3{\times}$ steeper than smooth-signal predictions) with reliable detection at $\alpha{\geq}0.75$.
9	The other four methods each fail in characteristic ways: naive cross-correlation inflates FPR to 75\%, differenced cross-correlation cannot separate forward from reverse precedence, threshold onset is power-limited at $T{=}10$ under $3\sigma$ calibration, and Toda--Yamamoto Granger provides supplementary detection at reduced power (directional assignment under verification).
10	
11	Applying permutation Granger, token entropy exhibits robust one-generation-ahead precedence over diversity collapse at $\alpha{=}1.0$ on WikiText-103 (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse across 4~seeds, replicated on GPT-2-medium and OPT-1.3B), with cross-architecture validation confirming forward detection (${\geq}6/8$) in 11 of 15 model-seed combinations spanning five families (345M--6.9B); non-confirming conditions span precision sensitivity (GPT-2 bf16), seed dependence (Pythia-1.4B), entropy decoupling (SmolLM3), and bidirectional confounding (GPT-2 s43).
12	Directional asymmetry is domain-dependent: consistent unidirectionality holds on WikiText across all regimes, while C4 exhibits near-symmetric bidirectional coupling at $\alpha{=}0.50$ (PermG reverse 7/8), though forward detection remains robust in both domains.
13	This finding is deliberately scoped to lexical diversity, not model collapse broadly, since the perplexity channel proved entirely spurious under differencing.
14	
15	The design considerations we expose arise from generic properties of short, non-stationary time series.
16	Cross-architecture (five families, 345M--6.9B), cross-scale ($3.4\times$ within Pythia), and cross-domain (C4) validation confirms that both the design considerations and the method recommendation are not specific to a single experimental configuration; evaluation on models at ${>}$7B parameters remains an open direction.
17	Limitations are detailed in \S\ref{sec:limitations}; future directions are discussed in \S\ref{sec:discussion}.
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01EuSu6K3Ab1cA1Fekw5yxJe`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Cascade Ordering}
2	\label{sec:cascade}
3	
4	If distributional collapse follows a predictable ordering across metrics, the earliest-responding metric can serve as a canary signal.
5	We formalize known metric properties---entropy as a continuous indicator of mode concentration, diversity as a threshold indicator of lexical coverage---under mode amplification~\cite{shumailov2024curse,dohmatob2024tale} (derivations in Appendix~\ref{app:cascade_theory}).
6	
7	\begin{designprinciple}[Entropy responds before diversity]\label{thm:entropy_leads}
8	Under mode amplification: \textup{(a)} entropy can decrease while the $\epsilon$-effective support $S_\epsilon(p) {=} |\{i: p_i > \epsilon\}|$ (hence distinct-$n$) is unchanged; \textup{(b)} if distinct-$n$ decreases, entropy has already decreased.
9	\end{designprinciple}
10	
11	The asymmetry arises because entropy responds to any mass redistribution (first-order), while diversity changes only when a token probability crosses the generation threshold~$\epsilon$; the margin $\gamma = \min_{i: p_i > \epsilon}(p_i - \epsilon)$ quantifies how much shift diversity absorbs silently.
12	
13	\begin{designprinciple}[Diversity lag $\geq 1$ generation]\label{prop:div_lag}
14	If the per-generation perturbation satisfies $\alpha\|\mathcal{T}(p_t) - p_t\|_\infty < \gamma(p_t, \epsilon)$ through entropy onset $t_H$, then diversity onset $t_D \geq t_H + 1$.
15	\end{designprinciple}
16	
17	ECE co-moves with entropy at zero lag: peakier conditionals simultaneously decrease per-token entropy and increase confidence ($\Delta\mathrm{ECE} \approx \Delta C > 0$ whenever $\Delta\bar{H} < 0$; qualitative argument in Appendix~\ref{app:cascade_theory}).
18	The predicted cascade $\{H, \mathrm{ECE}\} \to \mathrm{Distinct}$-$n$ holds in 14/15 experiments at $\alpha \geq 0.30$ (GPT-2-medium s43 bidirectional; within-domain WikiText, $2\sigma$ onset threshold): entropy and ECE onset at generation~1, distinct-$n$ at generation~2 ($t_H{=}t_{\mathrm{ECE}}{=}1$, $t_D{=}2$ uniformly; \S\ref{sec:regime}; Appendix~\ref{app:moment_cascade}).
19	Five low-$\alpha$ experiments show no detectable onset, leaving the ordering undefined below the detection threshold.
20	The assumption requires monotonic entropy decline; SmolLM3-3B (entropy 0/4; Table~\ref{tab:generalization}) provides an empirical counter-example, possibly due to architectural differences in attention or normalization.
21	This cascade establishes entropy as the earliest available canary and explains PermG's directional selectivity: forward tests detect temporal precedence (6/8) while reverse tests find none (0/8) at $\alpha \geq 0.50$.
22	
```

</details>

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01MLAxAAfVS8soa5hm8Ym3aY`</summary>

```text
1	\section*{Limitations}
2	\label{sec:limitations}
3	
4	Our landscape characterization uses Pythia-410M as the primary architecture; the phase diagram is Pythia-family-only (410M and 1.4B), and frontier-scale behavior ($>$7B) is untested.
5	With $T{\leq}25$ observations, statistical power remains limited ($\hat{\beta}{=}0.66$, CI $[0.24, 0.78]$); PermG's empirical FPR of $4/36$ (11.1\%, 6 seeds) is the lowest among directionally-specific methods but non-zero; the cluster-adjusted 95\% CI is $[1.2\%, 30.4\%]$ (within-seed ICC${=}0.14$).
6	Each cell's detection rate (e.g., 6/8) carries a wide binomial confidence interval (Clopper-Pearson: $[0.25, 0.92]$ for 6/8); the phase diagram should be read as a coarse topology rather than a precise probability surface.
7	Preliminary cross-architecture evaluation on GPT-2-medium reveals a substantially higher PermG FPR (6/12 pooled, 50\%), driven by ${\sim}4{\times}$ stronger null drift in entropy and ${\sim}7{\times}$ in distinct-3 (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_arch_fpr}); $K{\geq}3$ consensus maintains 0\% FPR across both architectures, though with a narrow margin (max 2/5 votes in the worst-case seed).
8	All experiments use full replacement~\cite{shumailov2024curse}; under data accumulation~\cite{dey2024breaking}, $\alpha_c$ would right-shift and the blind zone widen.
9	The sync-dip mechanism at $\alpha{=}0.60$ remains open.
10	All experiments use a single decoding strategy (temperature~$1.0$, top-$k{=}50$).
11	Lower temperatures accelerate mode concentration and should left-shift the detection boundary; nucleus sampling alters the effective diversity threshold $\epsilon$ in Design Principle~\ref{thm:entropy_leads}.
12	The directional effects are predictable---lower temperature compresses the landscape toward earlier detection---but the quantitative shift requires empirical calibration.
13	At boundary contamination levels ($\alpha \approx \alpha_c$), numerical precision (bf16 vs.\ fp32) can affect detection outcomes (GPT-2-medium: 0/8 bf16 vs.\ 6/8 fp32); all primary results use fp32 inference.
14	Per-condition replication (3--4 seeds) limits sampling robustness; the specification-curve analysis (1{,}160 configurations; Appendix~\ref{app:spec_curve}) partly compensates by systematically varying analytical choices.
15	Bootstrap confidence intervals at small sample sizes ($n{\leq}4$) should be interpreted with caution.
16	
```

</details>

```text
1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related_work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Model collapse and canary metrics.}
5	Iterative training collapse was formalized by \citet{shumailov2024curse} and \citet{alemohammad2024mad}, who showed that recursively replacing real data with model-generated text causes progressive tail erosion; \citet{hataya2023will} demonstrated analogous degradation in image generation; \citet{briesch2023large} confirmed diversity decline in self-consuming loops; \citet{dey2024breaking} showed that accumulation mitigates collapse; \citet{schaeffer2025position} systematize eight distinct notions of ``collapse'' that this literature conflates.
6	Our operationalization (declining detection rate of Granger-precedence canary$\to$diversity links across generations) aligns most closely with Schaeffer et al.'s \emph{distributional shift} family (definitions~D4--D6 in their taxonomy), measuring how the generated distribution departs from the real-data baseline rather than tracking population risk or scaling-law exponents.
7	Theoretical analyses have sharpened these results: \citet{dohmatob2024tale} recast collapse as a change in scaling-law exponents, \citet{dohmatob2025strong} proved that collapse can occur even at vanishingly small synthetic fractions, \citet{seddik2026statistical} provide a statistical analysis of iterative contamination dynamics, and \citet{marchi2025challenging} challenge the universality of collapse under diverse training conditions.
8	Several works have proposed monitoring metrics: \citet{du2025scales} report ECE increases at 30\% contamination before accuracy drops, \citet{gambetta2024surplexity} introduces surplexity as a collapse detector, \citet{drayson2025detection} show that machine-generated text detection can prevent collapse, \citet{li2025verification} propose verification-based mitigation, and \citet{feng2024beyond} show that even imperfect verifiers can prevent collapse at scale.
9	\citet{padmakumar2024writing} show that writing with feedback-tuned LMs reduces content diversity, a single-generation analogue of the iterative diversity loss we study.
10	\citet{shi2025closer} independently validate entropy as a collapse diagnostic in diffusion models ($r{=}0.91$ with generalization score), using it for diversity-aware sample selection.
11	\citet{du2026geometric} address a related but distinct phenomenon (mode collapse during generation) via geometric regulation of the representation trajectory; our work instead studies \emph{iterative training} collapse across generations.
12	Our work is complementary to AI-generated text detection~\citep{mitchell2023detectgpt}: whereas single-generation detectors classify individual texts as human- or machine-written, our approach tracks distributional shifts across multiple training generations, targeting the cumulative degradation process rather than the provenance of individual samples.
13	We differ from prior monitoring work in four respects (comparison table: Appendix~\ref{app:method_comparison}): (i)~we compare an entire family of canary metrics; (ii)~we cross-validate across five statistical methods; (iii)~we map detectability as a function of contamination regime; and (iv)~we treat method-selection sensitivity itself as a primary finding.
14	
15	\paragraph{Data mixing phase transitions.}
16	The observation that critical mixture ratios separate qualitatively different outcomes has precedent: \citet{kazdan2025collapse} map conditions under which synthetic data triggers collapse versus improves performance, and \citet{gu2024cmr} establish a scaling law for critical mixture ratios in continual pre-training.
17	\citet{gu2025datamixing} show that data mixing can induce phase transitions in knowledge acquisition, a direct theoretical analogue to our detection boundary $\alpha_c(T)$.
18	
19	\paragraph{Critical transitions and lead-lag methodology.}
20	\citet{scheffer2009early} established that systems approaching tipping points exhibit generic early-warning signals (rising autocorrelation and variance); \citet{scheffer2012anticipating} extended this framework to anticipating transitions before they occur; \citet{dakos2012methods} developed a practical toolkit for estimating such signals, while \citet{boettiger2012limits} quantified detection limits in short time series, directly relevant to iterative training where $T{=}5$--$20$.
21	\citet{truong2026phase} prove that entropy collapse in iterative training is a first-order (discontinuous) phase transition, structurally ruling out critical-slowing-down (CSD) early-warning signals.
22	Our Granger-precedence approach circumvents this limitation by detecting temporal predictive improvement rather than variance/autocorrelation signatures, achieving ${\geq}5/8$ forward detection in 12 of 15 above-boundary cells (three sync-dip exceptions at $\alpha{=}0.60$ dissolve by $T{=}25$; \S\ref{sec:t20}).
23	The standard framework for testing temporal precedence is Granger causality~\cite{granger1969investigating}, with neural extensions for nonlinear settings~\citep{tank2021neural,marcinkevics2022neural,zeng2025kangci,runge2019detecting}; however, non-stationary series inflate rejection rates~\cite{granger1974spurious}, and the Toda--Yamamoto procedure~\cite{toda1995statistical} addresses this at reduced degrees of freedom severe in short series.
24	We use classical linear PermG rather than neural Granger methods or PCMCI~\citep{runge2019detecting} because $T{\leq}25$ precludes reliable nonlinear conditional independence estimation.
25	These methods are largely absent from the model collapse literature.
26	Our analysis studies iterative from-scratch retraining; online or continual learning settings, where the model updates incrementally on streaming data, may exhibit different detection dynamics.
27	
28	\paragraph{Analytical robustness.}
29	\citet{steegen2016increasing} introduced multiverse analysis, \citet{bell2022modeling} brought it to ML, \citet{coretta2023multidimensional} demonstrated that 46 teams reached different conclusions on identical data, and \citet{kapoor2024reforms} synthesized these concerns into a consensus checklist.
30	Our work applies this perspective to model collapse: five methods across multiple contamination regimes produce conclusions ranging from universal significance to complete null.
31	
```

1	\section*{Ethics Statement and AI Writing Disclosure}
2	
3	\paragraph{AI writing assistance.}
4	This paper was drafted with assistance from Claude (Anthropic), used for LaTeX formatting, literature search, and iterative revision.
5	All scientific claims, experimental design decisions, data analysis, and interpretations are the sole responsibility of the authors.
6	The AI assistant did not design experiments, run code, or make editorial judgments about which results to include or exclude.
7	
8	\paragraph{Responsible NLP considerations.}
9	This work studies degradation in iterative language model training.
10	We do not release models trained on synthetic data, as they exhibit reduced diversity and calibration.
11	Our findings highlight a failure mode relevant to the responsible deployment of language models: unmonitored iterative training on web-crawled data (which increasingly contains LLM outputs) may degrade model quality in ways detectable only through the multi-method framework we present.
12	We encourage practitioners to implement canary monitoring in production training pipelines as a safeguard against silent quality degradation.
13

1	\appendix
2	
3	\section{Training Details and Metric Drift}
4	\label{app:training_details}
5	\label{app:drift}
6	
7	\paragraph{Hyperparameters.}
8	Each generation fine-tunes for 5{,}000 steps using AdamW (learning rate $5 \times 10^{-5}$, 500 warmup steps, weight decay $0.01$, batch size 4 for Pythia-410M, 32 for GPT-2-medium).
9	Generation uses temperature $1.0$, top-$k{=}50$, max length 512 tokens, producing 5{,}000 samples per generation from 5{,}000-sample subsets of WikiText-103.
10	Canary metrics: token entropy $H(p_\theta | \text{context})$ over 1{,}000 held-out samples; ECE with 15 bins.
11	Additional downstream metrics (distinct-3, effective rank, Self-BLEU) appear in subsequent appendix sections.
12	
13	\paragraph{Metric drift.}
14	
15	\begin{table}[h]
16	\centering
17	\small
18	\caption{Metric drift (\%, generation~0$\to$10) for Pythia-410M across contamination levels. Diversity collapse occurs at all $\alpha$ levels, with the strongest decline at low-to-moderate contamination ($\alpha{=}0.25$: distinct-1 $-40.3{\pm}0.3\%$) rather than full replacement ($\alpha{=}1.0$: $-27.8{\pm}1.6\%$). $^\dagger$$\alpha{=}0.10$: single seed (seed~42, preliminary). $\alpha{=}0.00$, $\alpha{=}0.25$, $\alpha{=}0.50$, $\alpha{=}0.75$: 3-seed mean${\pm}$std (seeds 42, 43, 44); $\alpha{=}1.0$: 4-seed mean${\pm}$std (seeds 42, 43, 44, 45).}
19	\label{tab:drift_summary}

25	0.00 & $-4.6{\pm}0.4$ & $+37.6{\pm}14$ & $-14.1{\pm}0.4$ & $-9.6{\pm}0.4$ & $-20.6{\pm}1.0$ \\
26	0.10$^\dagger$ & $-5.3$ & $+57$ & $-39.6$ & $-56.4$ & $-21.0$ \\

28	0.50 & $-18.0{\pm}0.5$ & $+822.6{\pm}18.3$ & $-37.1{\pm}0.4$ & $-50.4{\pm}1.0$ & $+21.0{\pm}0.1$ \\
29	0.75 & $-27.6{\pm}0.2$ & $+1791{\pm}18$ & $-32.3{\pm}0.5$ & $-41.0{\pm}1.0$ & $+182.6{\pm}0.7$ \\
